Like I was saying, I mostly just know I came in because my mind keeps going blank at the worst times. I don't think so.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself avoiding certain situations or tasks because of these blank moments?\n\nPatient: Yes. I... I stopped being able to do things. I don't really want to talk about that part.\n\nDoctor: Do the blank moments happen when you're reminded of your friend who passed away?\n\nPatient: I... I don't know. I haven't really noticed if it's connected like that. I just try not to think about it at all. So...
